## 1. Purpose and scope

The package exports configuration, HTTP, stream and cache utilities plus metocean, vessel, mooring and riser clients. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/__init__.py:L28-L49]
The routing registry assigns the enclosing data_systems domain beta maturity; the production-ready wording is a README claim, unverified. [docs/registry/module-routing.yaml:L99-L104] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/README.md:L4-L9]
The main route loads YAML configuration, queries or generates dictionaries, and serialises selected dictionaries as text; the mooring route selects generated components and runs capacity comparisons, rather than a line-equilibrium solve. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L100-L105] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L160-L182] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L196-L259]
The scope includes all four subpackages and the two legacy utility scripts; the download script has module-level calls, so inspection of that script is text-only. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/bsee_data_refresh.py:L5-L77] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/rig_name_matcher_openwells.py:L26-L45]
Do not use the approximate vessel response, catalogues, pressure screening or exported text as independently cleared design data; the implementation contains estimated hydrostatics, generated response functions and simplified pressure formulae. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L262-L308] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L296-L342] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L208-L217]

## 2. Inputs and outputs

### Inputs

| Input | Unit | Range / default | Route and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Configuration | dimensionless keys; timeout in s | Required `version`, `data_source`; unsupported versions warn; constructor does not itself call validation | `ConfigLoader._load`, `validate_schema`. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L45-L67] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L209-L229] |
| Query dates and location | datetime; latitude/longitude in degrees | Keys `lat`, `lon`; no common geographic range validator in query route; provider chooses defaults | `MetoceanClient.query_metocean`; Open-Meteo truncates forecast end date. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L137-L158] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/openmeteo_client.py:L91-L126] |
| Requested metocean quantities | wave height m, period s, directions degrees, speeds m/s | Default parameters are wave height and wind speed; provider keys differ | Query documentation and forwarding. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L149-L175] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/noaa_client.py:L187-L204] |
| Vessel dimensions, draft and deadweight | m; deadweight used as mass | Length and beam required; draft, depth and deadweight can be inferred or defaulted | `calculate_hydrostatics`; estimates require independent review. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L262-L294] |
| RAO query | draft m; angular frequency rad/s; direction degrees | Generated response grids; omitted frequency, heading and motion lists replaced by code defaults | `get_raos`, `_generate_generic_raos`, `_calculate_rao_for_motion`. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L175-L204] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L249-L271] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L279-L294] |
| Mooring request | design load kN; depth m | Catenary / taut_leg / semi_taut; default soil clay; safety factor from configuration | `design_mooring_line`; default factor values intentionally not reproduced. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L135-L179] |
| Component queries | chain/rope/pin diameter mm; anchor dry mass kg | Enumerated generated catalogues; nearest-size substitution possible | Chain and anchor getters. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/chain_db_client.py:L248-L284] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/anchor_db_client.py:L281-L314] |
| Pipe query and contents | nominal diameter inches; coatings mm and kg/m³; contents kg/m³ | Default schedule STD, grade X52; empty contents density default; nominal diameter is multiplied directly into an outside diameter | `RiserClient` and `PipeSpecificationClient`. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/client.py:L67-L98] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L174-L180] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L350-L408] |
| HTTP and metadata cache | timeout s; chunk bytes; TTL s | Retry policy in base client; memory enabled by default, Redis and disk opt-in | Base client and cache constructors. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/base_client.py:L118-L164] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L315-L338] |

### Outputs and conventions

| Output | Unit / representation | Limit and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Metocean records | dictionaries with datetime and provider fields | NOAA names wind and periods differently from the unified request names. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/noaa_client.py:L187-L204] |
| Bathymetry | positive depth m; land mapped to zero | Parse failure also returns zero elevation; zero is ambiguous. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L59-L77] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L109-L119] |
| AQWA adapter | dictionary with ISO timestamp and renamed wave/current fields | This function yields dictionaries, without assembling or loading a solver deck. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L288-L303] |
| Generic RAOs | motion/direction dictionaries of amplitude and phase arrays | Translations use response ratios; rotational comments specify deg/m; phases use degrees and periods are derived from angular frequency. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L249-L271] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L301-L342] |
| Hydrostatics | displacement labelled tonnes; lengths m | Output displacement is divided again by a mass conversion; independent unit reconciliation is required. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L270-L308] |
| Mooring dictionaries / YAML | lengths m, catalogued strengths kN; text strengths and EA converted to N | Synthetic nonlinear tension array remains labelled kN in the producer; exported unit consistency needs review. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/chain_db_client.py:L378-L405] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L391-L413] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L435-L465] |
| Pipe dictionaries / YAML | geometry mm/inches and m; mass kg/m; EA N; EI and GJ N·m² | Export uses submerged mass as mass per length; no unit-system declaration is emitted. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L227-L257] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L468-L500] |
| Stream summaries / extremes | same quantity units as supplied | Statistics accumulate a list; extremes fit Gumbel to all non-None records without an exposure-rate argument. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L390-L411] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L353-L379] |

UNVERIFIED: A common coordinate origin, wave heading sign and phase reference across providers and exported text have not been established; the vessel writer sets its own centre and direction fields, but these do not establish interoperability; issue pending (6). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L385-L423]

## 3. Method and governing standards

| Method | Implementing definitions | Basis and limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Configuration merge and substitution | `ConfigLoader._load`, `_inherit_config`, `_apply_preset`, `_substitute_env_vars`, `_deep_merge` | YAML safe-load, optional inheritance, root-only preset insertion, environment substitution and recursive override; missing inheritance/presets can be ignored. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L53-L159] |
| HTTP and cache | `RateLimiter.acquire`, `BaseAPIClient.get/post/stream_data/stream_lines`; cache get/set methods | Requests retries and token lists; cache errors may become misses and failed writes may only log. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/base_client.py:L57-L101] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/base_client.py:L151-L161] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/base_client.py:L200-L288] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L157-L205] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L237-L302] |
| Parsing and quality control | `FormatParser.parse_json_stream/parse_csv_stream/parse_netcdf_stream`; `QualityControl.range_check/filter_outliers/_is_outlier_iqr/_is_outlier_zscore`; `StreamHandler.aggregate_statistics` | JSON lines, delimiter splitting, h5netcdf, inclusive ranges, rolling sample statistics; insufficient windows and empty summaries use permissive results. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L56-L131] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L142-L234] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L378-L411] |
| Provider selection and extremes | `MetoceanClient.query_metocean/apply_quality_control/calculate_extremes`; NOAA `_find_nearest_buoy`; GEBCO `get_elevation` | Sequential fallback on exceptions; degree-space distance over a small station subset; parsed bathymetry; Gumbel quantile probabilities without observation frequency. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L175-L216] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L317-L379] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/noaa_client.py:L237-L252] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L109-L119] |
| Response generation and interpolation | `GenericRAOClient._calculate_rao_for_motion`, `_generate_generic_raos`, `interpolate_raos`; `VesselClient.calculate_hydrostatics/calculate_natural_periods` | Analytic response surrogates and interpolation; estimated mass and stability, empirical periods; these are not a diffraction solution or a hand-check reference for one. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L244-L342] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L363-L397] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L262-L348] |
| Generated component specifications | `ChainDatabaseClient._generate_chain_spec`; `WireRopeDatabaseClient._generate_rope_spec`; `SyntheticRopeDatabaseClient._generate_rope_spec/calculate_nonlinear_stiffness`; `AnchorDatabaseClient._generate_anchor_spec/calculate_holding_capacity`; `ConnectorDatabaseClient._generate_connector_spec/find_for_chain` | Diameter-based capacity and stiffness formulae, density assumptions, soil factors, a power-law rope extension and connector capacity criteria; no manufacturer qualification follows from a generated row. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/chain_db_client.py:L149-L215] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/wire_rope_db_client.py:L161-L213] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L186-L245] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L388-L413] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/anchor_db_client.py:L203-L252] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/anchor_db_client.py:L389-L425] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/connector_db_client.py:L183-L241] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/connector_db_client.py:L359-L377] |

## 5. QA gates

The gate set is Python calculations: G0 inputs/units/citations, G1 imports/tests, G2 hand or published check, G3 and G4 not applicable, G5 evidence (AQG r0, sections 9–11).
The following are executable review requirements; a passing regression is not a substitute for the outstanding provenance and programme-loading checks. ## 6. Known failure modes and pitfalls

Silent-return, record-loss, fallback and unit assumptions are listed first; a normal missing cache entry or intentional catalogue filter is distinguished from an error hidden as a successful result in the site inventory below. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L78-L110] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L109-L119]

| Symptom | Cause at the code pin | Detection check / incident / follow-up |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Partial or empty weather result reported as success | NOAA retrieval catches errors and returns normally; unified fallback stops after normal iterator exhaustion; records already yielded before an exception are not rolled back | no detection check exists; issue pending (4). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/noaa_client.py:L118-L159] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L188-L216] |
| Invalid observation disappears or malformed number aborts conversion | JSON parser skips malformed lines; NOAA accepts only one missing-value spelling and returns None after conversion errors; decimal/thousands separators are not normalised | no detection check exists; issue pending (3). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L66-L94] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/noaa_client.py:L173-L209] |
| Unknown depth appears as land | GEBCO parse failure returns zero; get_depth maps nonnegative elevation to zero | no detection check exists; issue pending (4). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L74-L77] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/gebco_client.py:L109-L119] |
| Wrong provider fields / coordinates / timestamps | Open-Meteo maps request names but reads original names; ERA5 reads original parameter keys, first grid location and hours-since-origin irrespective of decoded coordinate type; absolute download URL is passed into base URL concatenation | no detection check exists; issue pending (4), issue pending (5). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/openmeteo_client.py:L94-L105] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/openmeteo_client.py:L140-L162] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/era5_client.py:L173-L226] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/base_client.py:L216-L220] |
| Date coverage silently shortened or data omitted | Open-Meteo truncates forecast date; default empty hourly/time arrays yield no records; quality control permits absent/None fields; Gumbel default includes a nonfinite lower-tail quantile and omits sample frequency | no detection check exists; issue pending (4), issue pending (5). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/openmeteo_client.py:L107-L111] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/api_clients/openmeteo_client.py:L143-L162] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L317-L379] |
| Configuration appears loaded without intended parent/preset/version | Missing parent and preset only warn; missing environment values retain placeholders; unsupported versions still pass; README config and keyword examples drift | `config-schema` covers missing required keys only; no detection check exists for the remaining paths; issue pending (1). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L82-L109] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L122-L138] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/config_loader.py:L217-L229] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/README.md:L183-L199] |
| Cache failure looks like miss or successful write | Redis/disk catch errors; None is ambiguous; falsy TTL uses default; promotion resets age and can retain stale metadata | `cache-expiry` covers ordinary L1 expiry only; no detection check exists for tier failure/TTL/promotion ambiguity; issue pending (2). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L101-L107] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L145-L205] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L244-L302] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L389-L397] |
| Response/stability output implies unsupported accuracy | Unknown vessel type and lookup failure fall back; hydrostatics and periods use empirical constants, and nonpositive GM receives a fixed roll period; displacement scaling and radius dimensions need reconciliation | no detection check exists; issue pending (6). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L222-L236] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L262-L348] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/client.py:L459-L479] |
| Requested RAO interpolation is ignored or extrapolated | Draft change only warns, target directions unused; frequency interpolation extrapolates, unsupported motion returns zero arrays | no detection check exists; issue pending (6). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L335-L342] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/vessel/api_clients/generic_rao_client.py:L344-L397] |
| Incomplete line accepted or selection changes requested geometry | Missing ground chain/anchor can become None; validation only checks present anchors; nearest-size substitutions and unknown connector grades use fallback factors; lengths can become negative for shallow semi-taut inputs | no detection check exists; issue pending (7). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L329-L369] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L389-L435] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/client.py:L489-L510] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/connector_db_client.py:L295-L300] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/connector_db_client.py:L359-L365] |
| Catalogue grades or exported mass/stiffness use unsuitable basis | Legacy material view remains deliberate; nominal pipe size is used as actual OD; simplified pressures and submerged-mass export need qualification; synthetic EA includes an additional force scaling | `pipe-query`, `bare-mass`, `text-parse` are regression guards, not dimensional qualification; no detection check exists for these basis errors; #1147 and issue pending (7), issue pending (8). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L71-L76] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L174-L180] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L212-L217] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/riser/database_clients/pipe_db_client.py:L477-L488] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L199-L204] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/synthetic_rope_db_client.py:L443-L460] |
| Stream summary masks empty data and has unbounded accumulation | Empty mean/max/min/std become zero; summary/extremes retain all input values | no detection check exists; issue pending (3). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/stream_handler.py:L390-L411] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/metocean/client.py:L353-L365] |
| Unexpected downloads or unmatched identity rows | Legacy download calls execute at module scope; legacy matcher assumes populated text and aligned lookup lists | no detection check exists; issue pending (10). [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/bsee_data_refresh.py:L5-L23] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/rig_name_matcher_openwells.py:L15-L45] |

Metadata cache setters accept empty containers without a completeness policy; None is stored but is indistinguishable from a miss, and promotion carries values without the original remaining lifetime; no detection check exists for empty-result completeness; issue pending (2). ### Sweep site inventory

The grep is deliberately broad; the following inventory includes benign missing-value returns, intentional filters and internal constants as well as error paths, so its count is a count of reviewed sites, not a count of distinct defects. [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/common/cache_manager.py:L78-L110] [src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement/mooring/database_clients/chain_db_client.py:L426-L437]

```sh
rg -n --glob '*.py' 'except|continue|return None|return \[\]|return \{\}|\b(int|float)\(|\b1970\b|= 0(\.0)?\b|\.get\(|ttl = ttl or|density|degrees|rad/s|kg/m|kN|/ 1000|\* 1000' src/digitalmodel/data_systems/data_procurement
```

Every Python file was also parsed as syntax, without importing it: each exception handler, continue, empty/bare return, numeric conversion, fallback getter and numeric literal in executable definitions was examined; literal catalogue tables were reviewed in section 3 as data assumptions. ## 8. Ownership

Proposed clearance assignments: calculations family quality manager, independent-provider deputy quality manager, and data-pipeline maintainers as producing and remediation roles; no individual assignment or previous approval is claimed (AQG r0, sections 10–11).
Escalate ambiguous references, input conventions and changed tolerances to the family quality manager; unresolved clearance remains with the maintainers (AQG r0, sections 11–12).
Independent clearance is pending; role assignment and durable index registration require issue pending (12).

## 9. Dependencies

The package directly imports Requests for HTTP, YAML for configuration, NumPy/SciPy for calculations, and xarray/h5netcdf when parsing NetCDF; Redis is imported when its tier is enabled; pipe grades delegate to the materials package. ### Counted-family grep and classification

Search procedure: Python `re.search`, case-sensitive, each map `search_signals` pattern unchanged, over every package `.py` file at the code pin; exclude a pattern only if it matches `data_systems/data_procurement` case-insensitively; assign each line to its smallest enclosing function/class or the module statements. No signal was excluded for these three families.

| Family | Exact signal patterns |

### Map follow-up and whole-repository discovery

The package also implements configuration merging, HTTP rate limiting, metadata caches, JSON/CSV/NetCDF parsing, quality filtering, bathymetry parsing, Gumbel fitting, generated catalogue capacities and pipe section properties; these need explicit map families or clearer classification before their inventory can count in future reviews; issue pending (11).
